do\n  d=$(awk 'BEGIN{IGNORECASE=1} /^description:/ {sub(/^description:[[:space:]]*/,\"\"); print; exit}' \"$p\")\n  [ -z \"$d\" ] && d=\"No description provided\"\n  n=$(echo \"$p\" | cut -d/ -f1)\n  c=$(classify \"$n\")\n  printf '%s|%s|%s\\n' \"$c\" \"$n\" \"$d\"\ndone > \"$rows\"\n\nfor c in \"Engineering Process & Ops\" \"Game & Creative\" \"Web & Cloud\" \"Mobile & Apple Dev\" \"Productivity & Agent Tools\" \"Other\"; do\n  cnt=$(awk -F'|' -v c=\"$c\" '$1==c{n++} END{print n+0}' \"$rows\")\n  [ \"$cnt\" -eq 0 ] && continue\n  {\n    echo \"## $c ($cnt)\"\n    echo\n    awk -F'|' -v c=\"$c\" '$1==c {printf \"- `%s`: %s\\n\", $2, $3}' \"$rows\"\n    echo\n  } >> \"$out\"\ndone\n\nrm -f \"$rows\"\n\necho \"$out\""}

do\n  d=$(awk 'BEGIN{IGNORECASE=1} /^description:/ {sub(/^description:[[:space:]]*/,\"\"); print; exit}' \"$p\")\n  [ -z \"$d\" ] && d=\"No description provided\"\n  n=$(echo \"$p\" | cut -d/ -f1)\n  c=$(classify \"$n\")\n  printf '%s|%s|%s\\n' \"$c\" \"$n\" \"$d\"\ndone > \"$rows\"\n\nfor c in \"Engineering Process & Ops\" \"Game & Creative\" \"Web & Cloud\" \"Mobile & Apple Dev\" \"Productivity & Agent Tools\" \"Other\"; do\n  cnt=$(awk -F'|' -v c=\"$c\" '$1==c{n++} END{print n+0}' \"$rows\")\n  [ \"$cnt\" -eq 0 ] && continue\n  {\n    echo \"## $c ($cnt)\"\n    echo\n    awk -F'|' -v c=\"$c\" '$1==c {printf \"- `%s`: %s\\n\", $2, $3}' \"$rows\"\n    echo\n  } >> \"$out\"\ndone\n\nrm -f \"$rows\"\n\necho \"$out\""}

[470] tool exec_command call: {"cmd":"cd /Users/vincet/Documents/skills\nout=installed_skills_classified.md\n{\n  echo \"# Installed Skills (Classified)\"\n  echo\n  echo \"Generated: $(date '+%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S %Z')\"\n  echo\n} > \"$out\"\n\nclassify(){\n  n=\"$1\"\n  case \"$n\" in\n    ios-*|swiftui-*|android-*|macos-*|appkit-*|signing-*|packaging-*|swiftpm-*) echo \"Mobile & Apple Dev\" ;;\n    web-*|frontend-*|react-*|shadcn-*|supabase-*|stripe-*|cloudflare-*|workers-*|wrangler-*) echo \"Web & Cloud\" ;;\n    game-*|phaser-*|three-*|sprite-*|remotion-*|music-*|art-*|imagegen-*|creative-*) echo \"Game & Creative\" ;;\n    github-*|gh-*|circleci-*|code-review-*|repo-*|ci-*|deploy-*|launch-*|release-*|rollback-*|quality-*|qa-*|verification-*|runtime-*|reliability-*|stability-*|risk-*|ops-*|delivery-*|execution-*|automation-*|policy-*|trust-*|guardrail-*|safeguard-*|mission-*|control-*|throughput-*|triage-*|checkpoint-*|baseline-*|anomaly-*|drift-*|fail-*|recovery-*|bottleneck-*|cadence-*|priority-*|objective-*|impact-*|dependency-*|consistency-*|continuity-*|assurance-*|integrity-*|signal-*|stack-*|platform-*|workflow-*|command-*|dispatch-*|evidence-*|knowledge-*|learning-*|outcome-*|frontier-*|capability-*|decision-*|precision-*|confidence-*|variance-*|adaptive-*|action-*|experiment-*|handoff-*|feature-*|foundation-*|product-*|app-*|code-*|coding-*|crossdomain-*|cross-platform-*|fullcycle-*|fullstack-*|engineering-*|design-*|develop-*|discover-*|define-*|deliver-*|measure-*|iterate-*|review-*|standup-*|project-*|exec-*|research-*|utility-*|skill-*) echo \"Engineering Process & Ops\" ;;\n    documents-*|spreadsheets-*|presentations-*|browser-*|chronicle-*|mcp-*|agents-*|ai-agent-*|hardware-*|sandbox-*) echo \"Productivity & Agent Tools\" ;;\n    *) echo \"Other\" ;;\n  esac\n}\n\n# collect rows: category|name|desc\nrows=$(mktemp)\nfind . -maxdepth 2 -name SKILL.md | sed 's#^./##' | sort | while read -r p; do\n  d=$(awk 'BEGIN{IGNORECASE=1} /^description:/ {sub(/^description:[[:space:]]*/,\"\"); print; exit}' \"$p\")\n  [ -z \"$d\" ] && d=\"No description provided\"\n  n=$(echo \"$p\" | cut -d/ -f1)\n  c=$(classify \"$n\")\n  printf '%s|%s|%s\\n' \"$c\" \"$n\" \"$d\"\ndone > \"$rows\"\n\nfor c in \"Engineering Process & Ops\" \"Game & Creative\" \"Web & Cloud\" \"Mobile & Apple Dev\" \"Productivity & Agent Tools\" \"Other\"; do\n  cnt=$(awk -F'|' -v c=\"$c\" '$1==c{n++} END{print n+0}' \"$rows\")\n  [ \"$cnt\" -eq 0 ] && continue\n  {\n    echo \"## $c ($cnt)\"\n    echo\n    awk -F'|' -v c=\"$c\" '$1==c {printf \"- `%s`: %s\\n\", $2, $3}' \"$rows\"\n    echo\n  } >> \"$out\"\ndone\n\nrm -f \"$rows\"\n\necho \"$out\""}

do\n  d=$(awk 'BEGIN{IGNORECASE=1} /^description:/ {sub(/^description:[[:space:]]*/,\"\"); print; exit}' \"$p\")\n  [ -z \"$d\" ] && d=\"No description provided\"\n  n=$(echo \"$p\" | cut -d/ -f1)\n  c=$(classify \"$n\")\n  printf '%s|%s|%s\\n' \"$c\" \"$n\" \"$d\"\ndone > \"$rows\"\n\nfor c in \"Engineering Process & Ops\" \"Game & Creative\" \"Web & Cloud\" \"Mobile & Apple Dev\" \"Productivity & Agent Tools\" \"Other\"; do\n  cnt=$(awk -F'|' -v c=\"$c\" '$1==c{n++} END{print n+0}' \"$rows\")\n  [ \"$cnt\" -eq 0 ] && continue\n  {\n    echo \"## $c ($cnt)\"\n    echo\n    awk -F'|' -v c=\"$c\" '$1==c {printf \"- `%s`: %s\\n\", $2, $3}' \"$rows\"\n    echo\n  } >> \"$out\"\ndone\n\nrm -f \"$rows\"\n\necho \"$out\""}

do\n  d=$(awk 'BEGIN{IGNORECASE=1} /^description:/ {sub(/^description:[[:space:]]*/,\"\"); print; exit}' \"$p\")\n  [ -z \"$d\" ] && d=\"No description provided\"\n  n=$(echo \"$p\" | cut -d/ -f1)\n  c=$(classify \"$n\")\n  printf '%s|%s|%s\\n' \"$c\" \"$n\" \"$d\"\ndone > \"$rows\"\n\nfor c in \"Engineering Process & Ops\" \"Game & Creative\" \"Web & Cloud\" \"Mobile & Apple Dev\" \"Productivity & Agent Tools\" \"Other\"; do\n  cnt=$(awk -F'|' -v c=\"$c\" '$1==c{n++} END{print n+0}' \"$rows\")\n  [ \"$cnt\" -eq 0 ] && continue\n  {\n    echo \"## $c ($cnt)\"\n    echo\n    awk -F'|' -v c=\"$c\" '$1==c {printf \"- `%s`: %s\\n\", $2, $3}' \"$rows\"\n    echo\n  } >> \"$out\"\ndone\n\nrm -f \"$rows\"\n\necho \"$out\""}

[470] tool exec_command call: {"cmd":"cd /Users/vincet/Documents/skills\nout=installed_skills_classified.md\n{\n  echo \"# Installed Skills (Classified)\"\n  echo\n  echo \"Generated: $(date '+%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S %Z')\"\n  echo\n} > \"$out\"\n\nclassify(){\n  n=\"$1\"\n  case \"$n\" in\n    ios-*|swiftui-*|android-*|macos-*|appkit-*|signing-*|packaging-*|swiftpm-*) echo \"Mobile & Apple Dev\" ;;\n    web-*|frontend-*|react-*|shadcn-*|supabase-*|stripe-*|cloudflare-*|workers-*|wrangler-*) echo \"Web & Cloud\" ;;\n    game-*|phaser-*|three-*|sprite-*|remotion-*|music-*|art-*|imagegen-*|creative-*) echo \"Game & Creative\" ;;\n    github-*|gh-*|circleci-*|code-review-*|repo-*|ci-*|deploy-*|launch-*|release-*|rollback-*|quality-*|qa-*|verification-*|runtime-*|reliability-*|stability-*|risk-*|ops-*|delivery-*|execution-*|automation-*|policy-*|trust-*|guardrail-*|safeguard-*|mission-*|control-*|throughput-*|triage-*|checkpoint-*|baseline-*|anomaly-*|drift-*|fail-*|recovery-*|bottleneck-*|cadence-*|priority-*|objective-*|impact-*|dependency-*|consistency-*|continuity-*|assurance-*|integrity-*|signal-*|stack-*|platform-*|workflow-*|command-*|dispatch-*|evidence-*|knowledge-*|learning-*|outcome-*|frontier-*|capability-*|decision-*|precision-*|confidence-*|variance-*|adaptive-*|action-*|experiment-*|handoff-*|feature-*|foundation-*|product-*|app-*|code-*|coding-*|crossdomain-*|cross-platform-*|fullcycle-*|fullstack-*|engineering-*|design-*|develop-*|discover-*|define-*|deliver-*|measure-*|iterate-*|review-*|standup-*|project-*|exec-*|research-*|utility-*|skill-*) echo \"Engineering Process & Ops\" ;;\n    documents-*|spreadsheets-*|presentations-*|browser-*|chronicle-*|mcp-*|agents-*|ai-agent-*|hardware-*|sandbox-*) echo \"Productivity & Agent Tools\" ;;\n    *) echo \"Other\" ;;\n  esac\n}\n\n# collect rows: category|name|desc\nrows=$(mktemp)\nfind . -maxdepth 2 -name SKILL.md | sed 's#^./##' | sort | while read -r p; do\n  d=$(awk 'BEGIN{IGNORECASE=1} /^description:/ {sub(/^description:[[:space:]]*/,\"\"); print; exit}' \"$p\")\n  [ -z \"$d\" ] && d=\"No description provided\"\n  n=$(echo \"$p\" | cut -d/ -f1)\n  c=$(classify \"$n\")\n  printf '%s|%s|%s\\n' \"$c\" \"$n\" \"$d\"\ndone > \"$rows\"\n\nfor c in \"Engineering Process & Ops\" \"Game & Creative\" \"Web & Cloud\" \"Mobile & Apple Dev\" \"Productivity & Agent Tools\" \"Other\"; do\n  cnt=$(awk -F'|' -v c=\"$c\" '$1==c{n++} END{print n+0}' \"$rows\")\n  [ \"$cnt\" -eq 0 ] && continue\n  {\n    echo \"## $c ($cnt)\"\n    echo\n    awk -F'|' -v c=\"$c\" '$1==c {printf \"- `%s`: %s\\n\", $2, $3}' \"$rows\"\n    echo\n  } >> \"$out\"\ndone\n\nrm -f \"$rows\"\n\necho \"$out\""}
